    POST-8 — Pointwise samples of a degree-1 N1curl field depend on the MPI partition: ans:2's Row 1 moves −19 % … +112 % in SAR between -n 2 and -n 4, while every …¶

    Pointwise samples of a degree-1 N1curl field depend on the MPI partition: ans:2's Row 1 moves −19 % … +112 % in SAR between -n 2 and -n 4, while every integral agrees to ≈ 12 figures (opened 2026-09-27 by the 03:00 daily review from the 09-26 09:00 slot's disclosure and a log-pathologist ruling; known-issues 2026-09-27) | 🟡 Step 1 DONE 2026-09-27 (11:20 take-next; heavy-budgeted, 213 s / 174 s): scripts/probes/post8_pointwise_partition.py on 0b00091. Anchors green: P1 row reproduces its own width's logged row to 8.94e-11 (-n 2) / 4.95e-10 (-n 4); P1 phantom power within 4.80e-12 / 6.62e-14 of metrics.json:100. Mechanism reading: each named point has one incident cell; the raw N1curl E in it is width-invariant (16 point×drive SARs agree -n 2 vs -n 4 to ≤ 1.0e-9, print resolution), while the CG1 sample differs from it at both widths (+x, P1: N1curl 5.514140460e-07; CG1 4.858355042e-07 at -n 2, 5.878191741e-07 at -n 4, W/kg) and the cell's CG1 nodal |E|² sits at 0.83–0.98 of its own N1curl vertex value ⇒ the N1curl → CG1 nodal interpolation carries the dependence. The pre-registered "bracket" readout does not apply (one cell, no range). Census printed by text heuristic (18 candidate files; magnetostatic hits likely false positives — weekly to classify). 20260927T112115Z_POST-8-step1-n2.log, 20260927T112501Z_POST-8-step1-n4.log. Sampling rule: the weekly's. Original step text — Step 1 (§9 item 54, standard): a same-code control and a mechanism reading. Two probe windows of ans:2's case at -n 2 and -n 4 on one commit, with the tracked-artifact writes switched off. Asserted: each width reproduces its own logged Row 1 to 1e-9 relative, and the whole-phantom power is width-invariant to 1e-9. Printed: at each named point, the raw N1curl E in every incident cell next to both CG1 values. The Nédélec kernel is exact for this fixture's straight-sided tets, so the per-cell values are the partition-free ground truth. Also a census of which examples and gates sample points from an N1curl → CG1 interpolant. Done-when (§4): a sampling rule whose output is width-invariant to 1e-9 relative, asserted at -n 2 and -n 4 on ans:2's points, and adopted by every point-sampling site the census found. The rule is chosen by the weekly from step 1's reading (e.g. the owning cell by a deterministic global rule, or a DG0 cell average). Then ans:2's Row 1 is re-issued and the 09-26 ANS-2 pointwise relative level re-read. No band moves; no integral quantity is in question.

    §9 item 177 — 2026-10-02 (lane 1; the 09-30 weekly's sampling rule)¶

    Outcome (2026-10-02 15:00 slot): DONE — the rule implemented opt-in and gated at -n 1 / 2 / 4. All four windows Status 0, one tree.

    • Code: post.evaluation.point_owner_cells (owned candidates, int64 Allreduce(MIN) on original_cell_index, Allreduce(SUM) claim guard) and the keyword-only owner=None | "lowest_original_cell" on evaluate_vector_field_parallel; owner rides the OPS-40/55 allgather key; the legacy path is untouched (guard module 6 passed / 1 skipped at every width; C1a reproduces both logged CG1 rows).
    • Windows: W1 -n 2 20261002T200331Z_POST-8-step2-W1.log (195 s; 23 passed / 1 skipped); W2 -n 4 20261002T200654Z_POST-8-step2-W2.log (191 s); W3 -n 1 20261002T201013Z_POST-8-step2-W3.log (228 s); W4 real build 20261002T201406Z_POST-8-step2-W4-real.log (49 s; 17 passed / 5 skipped). git status --porcelain examples/ empty after W4.
    • K0: original_cell_index is an int64 ndarray covering owned + ghost cells in 0.11 (shape (223,) at -n 2, 191 owned + 32 ghosts); owned part unique, 384 = size_global. K1 / K2: CG1 ≤ 8.9e-16, N1curl ≤ 2.2e-15 (≤ 1e-12). K3: oracle incident sets 1 / 2 / 24 (vertex prediction 24 met); the rule returns the minimum 217 / 223 / 126 at all three widths, imaginary part 0. K4, K5, K6 green (K5 raises on every rank at -n 2 / 4, does not at -n 1). CK2: the legacy DG0 value at the vertex is in the incident set and is 126 (= min) at -n 2, 128 at -n 4, 131 at -n 1; at the facet 232 ≠ min at -n 4 — the legacy path's partition dependence shown on the known answer.
    • A0: 199 920 cells at every width; P1 phantom power rel 4.91e-12 / 8.93e-13 / 1.07e-11 (-n 2 / 4 / 1). A1: one owned containing cell per point at every width. A2 (the gate): max rel vs STEP1_N1CURL_SAR 1.37e-10 / 6.55e-11 / 3.03e-10 (≤ 1e-9). A3: owner-rank eval bitwise and all ranks bitwise at every width.
    • Cross-width record (not asserted): pairwise max relative difference of the 16 SARs: -n 2 vs -n 4 1.33e-10, -n 2 vs -n 1 1.66e-10, -n 4 vs -n 1 2.98e-10, all at P3 / −y (the smallest-magnitude entry class, 7.48e-9 W/kg). The printed prediction ≤ 1e-11 is missed (a negative result on a prediction, rule (e)); the asserted bound ≤ 2e-9 holds. The owner cell is the same (original index 150135 / 162464 / 142550 / 169932 at every width), so this is the solve's own width-dependent round-off at ≈ 1e-10 (P1 phantom power also moves 1e-11 across widths), not sampling.
    • C1a: 8.89e-11 (-n 2) / 5.89e-10 (-n 4) ≤ 1e-9. C1b: min |rel| vs the other width 0.1293 / 0.1485 ≥ 0.10. C1c (-n 1, printed): P1 CG1 row −3.43 / +87.8 / −4.30 / +29.8 % from the -n 2 row and −20.2 / +8.82 / −16.7 / +12.5 % from the -n 4 row (prediction ≥ 1 % from each: met). C1d: P1 +x CG1 / raw 0.881 (-n 2), 1.066 (-n 4; step 1's reading), 0.851 (-n 1). C2: owner local_to_global 85735 / 68816 / 194905 at +x for -n 2 / 4 / 1 while original_cell_index stays 150135 — even -n 1 renumbers, so local_to_global is not the input order at any width.

    Drafted by a read-only Plan agent and reviewed by the 2026-10-02 10:30 daily review.

    POST-8 step 2 puts the 09-30 weekly's sampling rule into the point-evaluation path, as an opt-in keyword on post.evaluation.evaluate_vector_field_parallel. The rule is gated on ans:2's named points at -n 1, -n 2 and -n 4. It also gets a known answer on a shared vertex and facet, and the CG1 route serves as the negative control. - Executor: implementer. Tier: heavy, since each ans:2 window is ≈ 2–5 min, above the 180 s standard tier. - Builds: complex, plus one cheap real-build window. - Cost: ≈ 4 measured (lane-1 median) / ≈ 50 predicted min. - Depends on: nothing; step 1 landed on 0b00091 (item 54).

    The rule as ruled (§10, 09-30 weekly): "Raw point evaluation takes a deterministic owner (lowest global cell index among the cells containing the point), gated width-invariant to 1e-9 at -n 1 / 2 / 4; public comparison rows quote cell or mass averages."

    Which global cell index: mesh.topology.original_cell_index, never index_map(tdim).local_to_global. - The original index is the cell's position in the input array given to create_mesh. Every birdcage mesh goes through _model_to_mesh (src/fem_em_solver/io/mesh.py:12), so that position is set by the gmsh mesh and not by the partition. - The index-map global number depends on the partition. Step 1 measured it: the same +x cell, carrying the same E digits, is global cell 85735 at -n 2 and 68816 at -n 4 (20260927T112115Z_POST-8-step1-n2.log:1918, 20260927T112501Z_POST-8-step1-n4.log:1920). The probe used local_to_global (scripts/probes/post8_pointwise_partition.py:102); do not copy that line.

    Code (a src/ change; rule (c) disclosure; additive only). In src/fem_em_solver/post/evaluation.py: - New public point_owner_cells(mesh, points, comm). It returns owner_original (int64[N], identical on every rank, −1 for a point outside the mesh) and owner_local_cell (int32[N]; the local cell on the owning rank, −1 elsewhere). 1. bb_tree → compute_collisions_points → compute_colliding_cells (the existing calls, :99–101). 2. Keep only owned candidates (c < size_local). 3. local_min = the minimum original_cell_index over those, with sentinel np.iinfo(np.int64).max. 4. Reduce with the buffer-form comm.Allreduce(local_min, global_min, op=MPI.MIN). 5. The rank whose local_min == global_min != sentinel owns the point. 6. Defensive: an Allreduce(SUM) of claim counts above 1 raises on every rank. - New keyword-only owner=None on evaluate_vector_field_parallel. - The accepted values are None (the legacy path, byte for byte) and "lowest_original_cell". Anything else raises ValueError before any collective. - Under the rule, each owning rank calls function.eval only on its owned points, in the owner cell. The existing gather / scatter / bcast (:120–135) is unchanged. - owner joins the OPS-40/55 key. The (n_points, digest) allgather becomes (n_points, digest, owner), so a rank-mismatched owner raises on every rank instead of hanging. - The default stays the legacy path. Every existing caller therefore stays digit-identical by construction: ≈ 30 test modules, core/solvers.py:763, post/sar.py:354, ports/gap_voltage.py:289, and the examples. Flipping the default is adoption, which is step 3. - Export point_owner_cells from src/fem_em_solver/post/__init__.py (additive).

    Module 1: tests/post/test_point_owner_rule.py — a known answer, cheap, run in both builds. - Fixture: create_unit_cube(comm, 4, 4, 4), with three points: interior (0.13, 0.27, 0.61), the hex-face plane (0.5, 0.3, 0.6), and the interior vertex (0.5, 0.5, 0.5). - Oracle, independent of DolfinX collision: allgather each owned cell's original index and its four vertices. Rank 0 takes the incident set at barycentrics ≥ −1e-12 and broadcasts it. - Asserted: - (K0) original_cell_index (the owned part, allgathered) is unique, and its count equals size_global. Print its type and shape: the 0.11 API may return a per-cell-type list, in which case take [0]. - (K1) CG1 (x, 2y, 3z) under the rule meets its closed form to ≤ 1e-12 at all three points (the guard module's bound, tests/post/test_evaluation_collective_guard.py:83). - (K2) N1curl a + b × x, with a = (1, −2, 0.5) and b = (0.3, 0.1, −0.2), meets its closed form to ≤ 1e-12. - (K3) The tie-break. A DG0 scalar holds original_cell_index in each cell (owned and ghost, then scatter_forward). Under the rule, each point returns exactly the minimum over the oracle's incident set, with imaginary part 0 in complex. The oracle count is 1 at the interior point and ≥ 2 at the facet and vertex points. - (K4) Every rank holds bitwise-equal values. - (K5) If rank 0 passes owner=None while the others pass the rule, every rank raises. At -n 1 the same construction does not raise (the control). - (K6) An unknown owner string raises ValueError. - Controls: - (CK1, asserted by construction once K3's count is ≥ 2 with distinct indices): the incident set's maximum ≠ its minimum at the vertex and facet points. - (CK2): the legacy value at the vertex is a member of the incident set (asserted). That it differs from the minimum at some width is predicted and printed only. - Printed: the incident-set sizes (predicted 24 at the vertex, not asserted) and the rule's three answers. The slot compares those answers across W1–W3.

    Module 2: tests/validation/test_post8_owner_rule_ans2.py — complex_only, one module-scoped fixture, width taken from comm.size. - Fixture: - sweep = build_four_port_sweep(phantom_resolution=PHANTOM_RESOLUTION_1G_RUNG), imported directly from tests.validation.test_port_birdcage_four_port and tests.validation.test_birdcage_sar_1g_rung. - _solve_driven(sweep, pid) for P1…P4, with the pids as _build_mass_averaged derives them (tests/validation/test_birdcage_sar_mass_averaged.py:232, 239–249), but without its 21 ball integrals. - Load ans:2's script by importlib, as the probe does (post8_pointwise_partition.py:77–82). Assert not ans2.RESOLUTION_OVERRIDDEN. - Use only ans2._pointwise_sar and ans2._phantom_power; never main(). - Import LOGGED_P1_ROW, LOGGED_P1_SOURCE, TRACKED_P1_POWER_W, ROW_RTOL and POWER_RTOL from the probe; never restate them. - Named points: (±CENTRE_RADIUS_M, 0, 0) and (0, ±CENTRE_RADIUS_M, 0), in ans:2's order (02_birdcage_coil_driven_sar_10MHz.py:747–755). - Anchors (asserted): - (A0) Cells == ONE_GRAM_RUNG_CELL_RECORD 199 920 exactly (n2 log :1901, n4 log :1903). P1 phantom power is within POWER_RTOL 1e-9 of TRACKED_P1_POWER_W; measured 4.80e-12 / 6.62e-14 (:1913 / :1915). - (A1) Each named point has exactly one owned containing cell globally (Allreduce SUM == 1; n2 log :1916 / 1930 / 1944 / 1958). - (A2) The gate. The 16 raw N1curl SARs σ|E|²/(2ρ) (with SALINE_SIGMA and PHANTOM_RHO_KG_PER_M3, as _pointwise_sar uses them), taken from evaluate_vector_field_parallel(e_complex, points, comm, owner="lowest_original_cell"), each lie within 1e-9 relative of STEP1_N1CURL_SAR. That record is restated with its log citation: - Each entry is the mean of the -n 2 print (n2 log :1917–1965) and the -n 4 print (n4 log :1919–1967). The two prints agree in 8 of 16 entries and differ by one unit in the 10th figure in the other 8. - Comparand budget ≤ 5e-10. Predicted measured ≤ 5e-10 at every width. - The -n 2 values (+x / +y / −x / −y): - P1: 5.514140460e-07, 8.056036637e-09, 6.147781056e-08, 1.000625322e-08; - P2: 5.413193615e-09, 5.365427637e-07, 7.538407567e-09, 6.062393526e-08; - P3: 6.887651145e-08, 7.937373888e-09, 5.038331027e-07, 7.475028492e-09; - P4: 9.595073748e-09, 6.651644441e-08, 9.294666807e-09, 4.980486020e-07. - Print with .17e: the 16 SARs and the E components, each point's owner original_cell_index and its local_to_global. - After W3 the slot records the pairwise maximum relative difference across the three logs, predicted ≤ 1e-11, as a record that is not asserted. Each window's assertion bounds that pairwise difference at ≤ 2e-9 through the common comparand; the docstring says so. - (A3) On the owning rank the rule's value equals e_complex.eval(x, [owner_local_cell]) bitwise, and every rank holds the same 16 values bitwise. - Negative control, the CG1 route (ans2._pointwise_sar on the same solves, which interpolates to CG1 and then uses the default owner=None): - (C1a, asserted, at -n 2 / -n 4 only): the P1 CG1 row reproduces LOGGED_P1_ROW[width] to ROW_RTOL 1e-9; measured 8.94e-11 / 4.95e-10 (n2 :1907, n4 :1909). This is also the check that the default path did not move. - (C1b, asserted, at -n 2 / -n 4): the same row against the other width's logged row has minimum |rel| ≥ 0.10. Measured −17.35 / −42.04 / −12.93 / −13.36 % (n2 :1908) and +20.99 / +72.54 / +14.85 / +15.42 % (n4 :1910). The minimum is 0.1293, i.e. 1.29e8× the 1e-9 the rule is held to. - (C1c, predicted, printed, -n 1): no logged -n 1 CG1 row exists. Print it beside both logged rows; predicted ≥ 1 % from each. - (C1d, printed): the CG1 / raw ratio per (point, drive). Step 1 read 0.881 / 1.066 at P1 +x. - (C2, predicted, printed): the owner's local_to_global differs across widths, while its original_cell_index does not.

    Traps: - Use the buffer-form Allreduce on int64 with MPI.MIN. The lowercase allreduce compares objects. Run pgrep -c python3 first, and never lengthen the timeout. - If the oracle's incident set disagrees with DolfinX's at the vertex, print both: that is a collision-tolerance finding. Never tune the 1e-12.

    The example's own assertions run unmodified inside step 4: that is (X). - What the spy records. It records a call only when sys._getframe(1).f_code.co_filename resolves to the example's path. src callers bound the real function at their own import, and anything else is forwarded unrecorded. For a recorded call, on every rank (the example's call is collective, OPS-40/55): 1. Forward the call unchanged; this is what the example receives. 2. Measure n_inc = _n_incident(msh, pts, comm) first. 3. own = real(f, pts, comm, owner=RULE) and leg = real(f, pts, comm, owner=None), on the identical pts array of this call. 4. owner_orig, owner_local = point_owner_cells(msh, pts, comm). 5. The legacy cell, from _dg0_index(msh) evaluated with owner=None (t2's _legacy_cells pattern; complex build: .real, assert .imag == 0). 6. At each multi-incidence probe, an in-cell evaluation on the owning rank, shared by buffer Allreduce SUM.

    Review ruling 2026-10-19 18:00 (item 548 blocked — diagnosis and re-pose)¶

    • Diagnosis (log-pathologist ruling; journal docs/planning/reviews/2026-10-19-daily-1800.md §4). The log is admissible: footer Status 1, 100 s (20261019T183519Z_POST-8-step3b-ii-e2-mag4-n2.log:755–758), with the same tuple on both ranks (:700–701, :732–733).
    • (1) CONFIRMED. The red is (S) at call 0, probe 20 (_drive_e2:410 → _check_e_s:204, :695–700), after all three rungs of the example ran to completion (:266, :445, :637, :678). Components 0 and 1 differ by exactly 1 ULP; component 2 is equal.
    • (2) Hypothesis A CONFIRMED from the code: within one call, owner and legacy evaluate different inputs on this mesh. two_torus_domain is partitioned with GhostMode.shared_facet (src/fem_em_solver/io/mesh.py:2306–2326, docstring :1371); birdcage_port_domain is too (:4911–4920). The legacy batch on a rank is every point that hits a local cell, owned or ghost, and the gather keeps the last rank's value (post/evaluation.py:379–388, :413–415). The owner batch is only the points whose owner cell is owned on that rank (:389–396). So on a ghosted mesh a single-incidence probe can be evaluated in a different batch (A1), or on a different rank in a ghost copy (A2). Which of these, or a ghost-copy coefficient difference (A3), produced probe 20's ULP is UNCOUNTABLE.
    • (3) Hypothesis B REFUTED (from code plus bytes). _n_incident counts owned copies, so n_inc = 1 means every ghost copy is a copy of the one cell. A different cell on the coarse rung would move components 0 and 1 (≈ 2 % of |B|, discretisation-dominated) by far more than 1 ULP.
    • (4) The (S) loop stops at its first red, but probe 20 is call 0's last index (21 points). So probe 20 is call 0's only single-incidence red. Calls 1–2, the multi-incidence checks and (N0) are unread. The journal's "other probes unknown" is corrected for call 0.
    • (5) mag:2 and every e1 site are ghost-free meshes (circular_loop_domain and the default partitioner, mesh.py:778–780). Their (S) greens are real for those meshes, but they cannot test A.
    • Ruling. The owner rule is not shown to be wrong. (S)'s premise, "call by call, same batch" (:870, :963), does not hold on a ghosted mesh, where owner and legacy are not evaluated in the same batch even on one call. How (S) is asserted on ghosted fixtures (bytes in a same-batch re-evaluation, an in-cell tolerance, or per-copy singletons) moves an assertion's form, so it is the weekly's. This review does not move it. ports:1 (two_torus_domain, examples/ports/01_two_torus_port_pair.py:416) and ports:12 (if it is built by birdcage_port_domain) are predicted to go red the same way.
    • Re-pose (rule (6), target unchanged). Split by mesh class:
    • Item 551 (e2a, attempt 2 of the e2 family since 549's gate): the ghost-free sites mag:2 and mag:6 under the unchanged (S). It also lands the e2 test code, with an e2-only checker that reports every red and the rank / ghost evidence.
    • Item 554 (e2b, attempt 3, depends on 551): the ghosted sites mag:4, ports:1 and ports:12. Each runs its (X) at record width and the separating measurement, with (S) pre-registered as a predicted red. It hands the (S) form to the weekly with a full red census.
    • The parked branch stays until item 554 lands. Item 551 takes the module and two script edits from it by path, and item 554 takes the other three.
    • Family count. 548 was attempt 1, 551 is attempt 2 and 554 is attempt 3, against a cap of 4. A green 551 lands a gate (the §7 state line moves), which resets the count.
